- **CONTRIBUTION**: `src/agents/` owns two things: the agent record and its dashboard CRUD, and the chat runtime that runs an agent's turn (history -&gt; LLM -&gt; tools -&gt; LLM -&gt; saved reply). - **contribution-guard**: Read-only code reviewer that checks changes against project coding rules and contribution guidelines. Never pushes code or creates PRs. Delegate to this agent for thorough pre-submission review. ([dougwithseismic/workflow-toolkit](https://github.com/dougwithseismic/workflow-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/contribution-guard.md) · ★1)
- **contribution-ranker**: Adversarially validates and ranks Contributor Scout candidates. Tries to disprove every finding, assigns dispositions, applies the scoring rubric via the deterministic script, shortlists at most three candidates, and recommends one primary. Prefers concluding that no contribution meets the threshold over shortlisting weak findings. Read-only. ([Phantom-IN/contributor-scout](https://github.com/Phantom-IN/contributor-scout/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/contribution-ranker.md) · 3 copies)
